National Cannabis and Hemp Business Directory — Independent Fact-Check Audit and Corrected Report

Last audited: 23 October 2026

This document has two parts. Part 1 is the audit of all 251 businesses in the original directory (sources accessed 2026-10-23 in the original). Part 2 is the complete corrected rewrite: every confirmed business and all its original details are kept, corrections from Part 1 are applied in place under each entry as an “Audit correction” line, businesses marked Removed are taken out (14), and businesses marked Unverified are kept but flagged [UNVERIFIED]. Every business in Part 2 keeps its source link(s).

Method (Part 1). Each business was checked against its official website first (including a live HTTP check of all 251 listed websites on 2026-10-23, redirects followed), then a reliable public source where needed — SEC filings, state business/insurance/banking regulators, acquirer or parent-company releases. HTTP 403/405/406/999 responses are bot walls and were treated as “site live”, not broken links; HTTP 000 timeouts were re-checked by search before any conclusion. A search snippet alone was never treated as confirmation of a contact detail. Only contact details the business itself publishes are used; no personal phone numbers or emails of individuals are reproduced — where the original report used a named individual's contact as a business contact, Part 1 flags it for removal without reproducing it. Credentials: the original directory lists almost no licence numbers; every licence/certification claim that does appear was checked against the official lookup named in its row, and is marked Confirmed, Corrected or Unverified there.

PART 2 — CORRECTED REPORT (complete rewrite)

Last audited: 23 October 2026 · 237 businesses (251 audited − 14 Removed) · Categories 1–10

Scope, as commissioned and unchanged: national and multi-state businesses that (a) are built around cannabis/hemp or (b) actively serve cannabis/hemp clients, with evidence shown. State-licensed cannabis operators are out of scope and belong in the separate licence database, except Category 9, which lists national brands and brand parents, not licences — entries the audit found to be licensed operators in substance (Nabis, Eaze) are flagged in place for cross-reference to the licence database. Contact abbreviations: W = website, P = business phone, E = business email, HQ = headquarters, Soc = social media, NP-V = Not published – Verify. Only public business contact details are given, never personal contacts.

Source links: every entry keeps its original source link(s) where the original gave them. Where the original entry gave no source link — 77 entries, mostly Cat 4 and Run 2 rows whose source cell cited a category-level directory or “no direct index hit opened” — the source given is the official-site / authority URL the audit itself checked for that business, taken from Part 1’s “What was checked” column.

How corrections are applied: the original entry text is kept in full. Where Part 1 corrected a field, an Audit correction (23 Oct 2026) line directly under the entry states the corrected value, which supersedes the original field above it. Where Part 1 could not verify the business, the entry heading carries [UNVERIFIED] and the audit line states what could not be confirmed and where it could be.

  • States served / scope: National retail
  • Key people: People NP-V — Verify
  • Source(s): https://hempz.com
  • Audit correction (23 Oct 2026) — Corrected: HQ is WRONG in the original: Dallas, TX (15770 North Dallas Parkway, Dallas, TX 75248) — not Sherman, TX (no source supports Sherman). Parent/owner omitted in the original: TSG Consumer Partners (acquired 1 Apr 2020, PE-backed). (An earlier audit note attributing Hempz to "Topanga Skincare / Pacific World, Aliso Viejo" was not sustained against PitchBook/Mergr — do not use it.) Hemp connection fully evidenced: every product is formulated with 100% pure hemp seed oil, THC/CBD-free, since 1998 (official site) — state that scope plainly. The original's "largest US hemp body-care brand" superlative was not independently verified — Hempz's own site claims "#1 hemp seed oil beauty and wellness brand"; treat the superlative as the brand's own claim. People remain NP-V (original correct — none published). No HQ address/phone/email was found published on hempz.com itself; the Dallas HQ rests on consistent independent secondary sources (PitchBook, Mergr). Product attributes (vegan, cruelty-free, gluten-free, paraben-free) are self-stated on the official About page, not third-party certifications verified here. Still sold nationally. (Checked: hempz.com (200, incl. About Us and About Hemp Seed Oil pages); PitchBook profile; Mergr)

  • Description: Kansas industrial hemp processor (decortication) supplying fibre, hurd and grain products to manufacturers in multiple states

The name trap resolves in the original's favour: HQ is Great Bend, KS — despite the "South Bend" name (South Bend is the local township/area name, not an Indiana location). Legal name South Bend Industrial Hemp Processing LLC. First hemp decortication facility in Kansas (opened June 2021), dual-purpose grain+fibre; now ~27 growers across Kansas, ~7,000 acres, plus growers in Oklahoma and Nebraska and a processing partnership with Dakota Hemp LLC (South Dakota) — still operating and growing per Oct 2026 reporting.
